</div></div><div data-onenote-x="2.6582677364349365" data-onenote-y="12.35531997680664" data-onenote-w="13" data-onenote-h="0.6000000238418579"><div class="container-outline" style="max-width: 624px"><div class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 0px"><p style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt">type Reader interface {</p></div>
<div class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 0px"><p style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt">Read(p []byte) (n int, err error)</p></div>
<div class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 0px"><p style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt">}</p></div>
</div></div><div data-onenote-x="7.658267498016357" data-onenote-y="12.35531997680664" data-onenote-w="13" data-onenote-h="0.6000000238418579"><div class="container-outline" style="max-width: 624px"><div class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 0px"><p style="font-family: Microsoft YaHei,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt">对于要用作读取器的类型，它必须实现 io.Reader 接口的唯一一个方法 Read(p []byte)。 换句话说，只要实现了 Read(p []byte) ，那它就是一个读取器。</p></div>
</div></div><div data-onenote-x="16.658267974853516" data-onenote-y="17.85531997680664" data-onenote-w="13" data-onenote-h="0.6000000238418579"><div class="container-outline" style="max-width: 624px"><div class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 0px"><p style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt">func Open(name string) (file *File, err error)</p></div>
</div></div><div data-onenote-x="15.658267974853516" data-onenote-y="19.35531997680664" data-onenote-w="13" data-onenote-h="0.6000000238418579"><div class="container-outline" style="max-width: 624px"><div class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 0px"><p style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt">func Create(name string) (file *File, err error)</p></div>
</div></div><div data-onenote-x="21.658266067504883" data-onenote-y="20.85531997680664" data-onenote-w="8.986112594604492" data-onenote-h="0.6000000238418579"><div class="container-outline" style="max-width: 624px"><div class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 0px"><p style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt; padding-bottom: 16px"><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: Monaspace Neon,sans-serif; font-size: 10pt">flag</span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: -apple-system,sans-serif; font-size: 12pt"> 可以选下列的值:</span></p></div><ul class="list-0" style="position: relative"><li class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 36px"><span style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt"><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: Monaspace Neon,sans-serif; font-size: 10pt">os.O_RDONLY</span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: -apple-system,sans-serif; font-size: 12pt">：只读</span></span></li>
<li class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 36px"><span style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt"><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: Monaspace Neon,sans-serif; font-size: 10pt">os.O_WRONLY</span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: -apple-system,sans-serif; font-size: 12pt">：只写</span></span></li>
<li class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 36px"><span style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt"><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: Monaspace Neon,sans-serif; font-size: 10pt">os.O_RDWR</span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: -apple-system,sans-serif; font-size: 12pt">：读写</span></span></li>
<li class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 36px"><span style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt"><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: Monaspace Neon,sans-serif; font-size: 10pt">os.O_APPEND</span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: -apple-system,sans-serif; font-size: 12pt">：追加写</span></span></li>
<li class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 36px"><span style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt"><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: Monaspace Neon,sans-serif; font-size: 10pt">os.O_CREATE</span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: -apple-system,sans-serif; font-size: 12pt">：如果文件不存在则创建</span></span></li>
<li class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 36px"><span style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt"><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: Monaspace Neon,sans-serif; font-size: 10pt">os.O_TRUNC</span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: -apple-system,sans-serif; font-size: 12pt">：打开时清空文件</span></span></li>
<li class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 36px"><span style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt"><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: Monaspace Neon,sans-serif; font-size: 10pt">os.O_EXCL</span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: -apple-system,sans-serif; font-size: 12pt">: 和 </span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: Monaspace Neon,sans-serif; font-size: 10pt">os.O_CREATE</span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: -apple-system,sans-serif; font-size: 12pt"> 配合使用，文件必须不存在</span></span></li>
<li class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 36px"><span style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt"><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: Monaspace Neon,sans-serif; font-size: 10pt">os.O_TRUNC</span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30); font-family: -apple-system,sans-serif; font-size: 12pt">: 打开时清空文件</span><span style="color: rgb(32,31,30)">> </span></span></li>
</ul>
</div></div><div data-onenote-x="11.658267974853516" data-onenote-y="21.35531997680664" data-onenote-w="13" data-onenote-h="0.6000000238418579"><div class="container-outline" style="max-width: 624px"><div class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 0px"><p style="font-family: Calibri,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt">func OpenFile(name string, flag int, perm FileMode) (file *File, err error)</p></div>

<div class="outline-element" style="margin-left: 0px"><p style="font-family: Microsoft YaHei,sans-serif; font-size: 11pt">继承性：子进程会继承父进程的环境变量 字符串格式：键和值都必须是字符串（Key=Value） 生命周期：随进程创建而加载，进程结束而销毁</p></div>
